# Commands · H · part 1

1,489 entries, h to history. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **h**: $ARGUMENTS ([bearingfruitco/cc-boiler-js-public](https://github.com/bearingfruitco/cc-boiler-js-public/blob/HEAD/.claude-backup-before-cleanup-20250131/commands/h.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **h-archive**: Archive STANDARD/RESEARCH task at Phase 6/R3 — gate → WAL → move → DONE. TRIGGER: '归档' / Phase 6 boundary. NOT FOR: PATCH (rejected); PR (use /h-pr). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-archive.md) · ★14)
- **h-brief**: Scaffold a new task_brief from schema + bidirectional bind to launch_spec ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-brief.md) · ★14)
- **h-ci**: Ingest CI/CD failure → classify → route (debug task / incident) + failure_memory record. TRIGGER: red GitHub Actions run / 'CI 挂了'. NOT FOR: local test fail (use /h-fix-bug). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-ci.md) · ★14)
- **h-collab**: Generate a cross-team collaboration deliverable from task_brief — draft document, create collab state file, mark task pending external review ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-collab.md) · ★14)
- **h-collab-update**: Log external team feedback on a collab deliverable — record questions/answers, update open items, optionally sign off to unblock Implement ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-collab-update.md) · ★14)
- **h-context-check**: Pull-model context probe — recent events + recurring failures + active task + dirty diff + sensitive-surface scan + active insights. The canonical entry to gather context before deciding profile / scope / next action. TRIGGER: phase start / 不确定状态 / 进入未知领域. NOT FOR: every prompt (this is on-demand, not push). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-context-check.md) · ★14)
- **h-decompose**: Decompose PRD/EPIC → INVEST subtasks + per-task briefs + launch_spec rows. TRIGGER: multi-feature input / '拆分需求'. NOT FOR: single task (use /h-brief). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-decompose.md) · ★14)
- **h-design**: Design step for HIGH-risk brief — dispatch system-architect, write ADRs, fill §8/§9. TRIGGER: HIGH-risk after /h-brief / '做设计'. NOT FOR: implementation. ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-design.md) · ★14)
- **h-distill**: Wiki cleanup — librarian scan → user approves → execute. TRIGGER: explicit user request ('整理 wiki' / 'clean stale wiki') or after `distill_threshold.py` reports growth thresholds tripped. NOT FOR: doc cleanup (use documentation-curator). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-distill.md) · ★14)
- **h-distill-from-code**: Reconcile wiki claims against current code, bounded scope. TRIGGER: 'wiki 过时了 校对一下' / 'reconcile'. NOT FOR: WAL at Archive (use /h-archive). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-distill-from-code.md) · ★14)
- **h-evolve**: Turn a high-confidence insight into a concrete rule-change proposal. Pick insight → generate file/line edit suggestion → human approves → optional --apply. TRIGGER: agent or user wants to act on an active insight. NOT FOR: routine insight viewing (use /h-context-check). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-evolve.md) · ★14)
- **h-fix-bug**: Bug → root-cause-first pipeline; production bugs get incident record. No code until root cause locked. TRIGGER: bug report / 'P1 down'. NOT FOR: CI fail (use /h-ci). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-fix-bug.md) · ★14)
- **h-from-ticket**: Ingest GitHub Issue / Jira / Linear ticket → task_brief + ambiguity check + launch_spec row. TRIGGER: ticket URL pasted. NOT FOR: PRD/EPIC (use /h-decompose). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-from-ticket.md) · ★14)
- **h-gates**: Full gate suite audit across accumulated diff — phase-aware, severity-aggregated. TRIGGER: phase transition / '过门'. NOT FOR: per-edit (hook handles). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-gates.md) · ★14)
- **h-help**: Scenario-driven menu of /h-* commands. Use when you're not sure which command fits your current situation. TRIGGER: user asks "which command should I use" / "help" / "怎么用" / new user onboarding. NOT FOR: detailed how-to of a specific command (read that command's own file). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-help.md) · ★14)
- **h-incident**: Capture production incident → wiki/incidents/&lt;date&gt;_&lt;slug&gt;.md with future-LLM reminder line. TRIGGER: Sentry alert / '记录事故'. NOT FOR: bug pipeline (use /h-fix-bug). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-incident.md) · ★14)
- **h-pr**: Create a pull request from the current task — pre-gate, build PR body from task_brief, gh pr create, write PR number back to launch_spec ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-pr.md) · ★14)
- **h-publish-insight**: Manually convert a local insight into a git-tracked team knowledge doc under .claude/wiki/insights/. Bridges single-machine expert system → team-shared learning. TRIGGER: user explicit invoke ONLY (never auto-fired). NOT FOR: rule changes (use /h-evolve), routine viewing (use /h-context-check). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-publish-insight.md) · ★14)
- **h-reflect**: Session reflection — multi-select lessons (incident/wiki/failure/success/memory) → reset counters. - **h-research**: Scaffold research_report.md (no code) — Phase R1 entry. TRIGGER: prompt contains research verb (analyze / feasibility / evaluate / 调研) / @research shortcut. - **habits**: Habit tracking. First run interviews you one question at a time to build a versioned habits profile — each habit with its own criteria (daily / N-per-week / N-per-month, build or limit) and a priority. Day-to-day tracking lives in dated markdown files (life/habit-tracking/&lt;date&gt;.md) you tick like a checklist; a SQLite store is synced from them for analysis. Afterward it shows your progress vs each criteria (✅ met / ⏳ not yet / ⚠️ over), top 20 by priority. Habits are auto-marked from your journaling + planning sessions. Once your diet + fitness profiles exist, the scored defaults "Eat clean"… ([baymac/pbrain](https://github.com/baymac/pbrain/blob/HEAD/commands/habits.md) · ★1)

- **hermes-adopt**: Onboards an existing codebase into the Hermes SDLC. Reads the entire repo, infers stack and current state, produces a gap report, generates CLAUDE.md, and populates Taskmaster with tasks derived from the current project state. Run with /hermes-adopt from the project root. ([A-Director/cc-forge](https://github.com/A-Director/cc-forge/blob/HEAD/commands/adopt.md) · ★2)
- **hermes-argus**: Framework self-check. Verifies Layer 1 (plugin) and Layer 2 (project state) integrity per spec §5. Stratified drift report (E-1), C-1 intake reconciliation, versioned --json output (E-2), banner-rendering approximate caveat (E-3), freshness-checked Layer-2 cache (§2.7). Read-only by default; --fix applies a conservative set of safe auto-fixes (§5.5). ([A-Director/cc-forge](https://github.com/A-Director/cc-forge/blob/HEAD/commands/argus.md) · ★2)
- **hermes-audit**: Run a full Hermes council audit on the current repo ([A-C-I-SOFTWARE-AND-DEVELOPMENT/M.U.S.E](https://github.com/A-C-I-SOFTWARE-AND-DEVELOPMENT/M.U.S.E/blob/HEAD/dotclaude/commands/hermes-audit.md) · ★3)
- **hermes-backlog-init**: Initialises the cc-forge product backlog for a project. Reads the confirmed stack and project scope from .cc-forge/state.json and CLAUDE.md, copies the default domain catalogues, marks universal items as active, marks stack-specific items active/not-applicable based on stack, and generates domain-specific DoD. Run automatically after hermes-init or hermes-adopt. Can also be run standalone with /hermes-backlog-init. ([A-Director/cc-forge](https://github.com/A-Director/cc-forge/blob/HEAD/commands/backlog-init.md) · ★2)
- **hermes-build-plan**: Create a staged implementation plan for the current task using all relevant agents ([A-C-I-SOFTWARE-AND-DEVELOPMENT/M.U.S.E](https://github.com/A-C-I-SOFTWARE-AND-DEVELOPMENT/M.U.S.E/blob/HEAD/dotclaude/commands/hermes-build-plan.md) · ★3)
- **hermes-dashboard**: Generates status/dashboard.html — a single self-contained HTML view of backlog completion, risks, decisions, and Hermes framework usage. Markdown stays source of truth; the dashboard is a read-only regeneratable view. Run after phase transitions, mid-project sanity checks, or before major reviews. ([A-Director/cc-forge](https://github.com/A-Director/cc-forge/blob/HEAD/commands/dashboard.md) · ★2)
- **hermes-deploy**: Run the production deploy sequence. Pre-flight checks, persona gate verification, Railway deploy, and post-deploy verification. ([A-Director/cc-forge](https://github.com/A-Director/cc-forge/blob/HEAD/commands/deploy.md) · ★2)
- **hermes-gate-review**: Trigger an SDLC gate review. Reads the current stage, determines which personas are due, invokes them as forked subagents, and produces a consolidated gate report. Run with /hermes-gate-review. ([A-Director/cc-forge](https://github.com/A-Director/cc-forge/blob/HEAD/commands/gate-review.md) · ★2)

- **hire-assess-fit**: Run a culture-add fit assessment for a candidate (NOT culture-fit). Names what the team currently lacks, names what this candidate adds, runs gap-bridge analysis. ([frankxai/Starlight-Intelligence-System](https://github.com/frankxai/Starlight-Intelligence-System/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/hire-assess-fit.md) · ★8)
- **hire-calibrate**: Run the pre-loop calibration session — 60 minutes with ≥3 raters, review the rubric, score 2 anchor candidates the team has previously seen, surface inter-rater drift, agree on hire-bar examples. The calibration session itself is the deliverable. Without it, the loop runs on uncalibrated rulers. Per Project Oxygen — cross-rater alignment beats rater quality. Not legal advice. ([frankxai/Starlight-Intelligence-System](https://github.com/frankxai/Starlight-Intelligence-System/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/hire-calibrate.md) · ★8)
- **hire-debrief**: Run the post-loop decision debrief — facilitator script for the 45-60 minute session. Surface inter-rater drift, anchor every claim to the rubric, name bias patterns out loud, render the hire-or-no-hire decision per the pre-committed rule, and capture learning for the next loop. Structured scores submitted BEFORE discussion (kills loud-voice halo). Tie-breaks default to hire-no. Not legal advice. ([frankxai/Starlight-Intelligence-System](https://github.com/frankxai/Starlight-Intelligence-System/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/hire-debrief.md) · ★8)
- **hire-decision**: One-call: compose a stack, fetch reputation per agent, recommend the top item by composite score ([oliver-pringle/acp-find-plugin](https://github.com/oliver-pringle/acp-find-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/hire-decision.md) · ★1)
- **hire-design-interview**: Generate the structured interview architecture for a role — per-slot interviewer briefs, dimensions, question stems with rationale, behavioral anchors at 1/3/5, time allocation, and the cognitive-ability or work-sample slot per Schmidt & Hunter's validity bundle. Refuses unstructured interviews and personality-as-primary. Not legal advice. ([frankxai/Starlight-Intelligence-System](https://github.com/frankxai/Starlight-Intelligence-System/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/hire-design-interview.md) · ★8)

- **history**: Manage the native session-transcript store for this repo by invoking the abcd binary. list, show and staged are read-only; capture, drain and ingest are the redacting write paths, migrate repairs records in place, reconstruct renders one session as an artefact plus telemetry, and discard permanently deletes one unredacted staged or quarantined transcript. list --session reaches one session's whole set — its main thread and every sub-agent it spawned. The store is user-level, keyed on the repo's root-commit SHA, and every stored transcript is redacted on write. ([intentdriven/abcd](https://github.com/intentdriven/abcd/blob/HEAD/commands/history.md) · ★3)
